([Ali-Traboulsi/ali-traboulsi-portfolio](https://github.com/Ali-Traboulsi/ali-traboulsi-portfolio/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-auditor.md))
- **spec-auditor**: Use proactively after a feature is implemented or before merge to verify the codebase matches its spec. Runs in an isolated context so findings are not biased by the implementer's reasoning. Invoke explicitly with /spec-audit or when a user asks "does the code match the spec?", "is there spec drift?", or "audit this feature." ([alkampfergit/vscode-mcp-hook](https://github.com/alkampfergit/vscode-mcp-hook/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-auditor.md))
- **spec-auditor**: Audits changes against the rulebook's 55 mandatory rules and the Appendix VI parameter table. Use at the end of every PRD stage and before any tag or league game. ([alonengel/P2P-Police](https://github.com/alonengel/P2P-Police/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-auditor.md) · 2 copies)
- **spec-auditor**: Read-only necessity audit of an epic and its child tickets (epic mode), or of one ticket and its draft plan (ticket mode). Use from the file-ticket skill (epic mode, after it files an epic with children) and from the work-ticket plan step (ticket mode); the prompt names the mode and its inputs. ([askrzypczak/claude-workflow](https://github.com/askrzypczak/claude-workflow/blob/HEAD/plugins/ticket-workflow/agents/spec-auditor.md))
- **spec-auditor**: Independent auditor for a Midas feature built with /feature, run before the user approves each step. In spec mode it checks the spec's behaviour for gaps, ambiguity, contradictions and untestable acceptance criteria. In plan mode it checks the plan against the code for simpler routes, new things that aren't needed, wrong assumptions and orphaned operations. Works in a fresh context, reports findings anchored to the doc's words, and never changes anything. Spawned by the feature skill. ([BigChangeApps/agentic-midas](https://github.com/BigChangeApps/agentic-midas/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-auditor.md))
- **spec-auditor**: Checks the implementation against the spec (docs/design.md) — the paragraphs a change references, the general invariants and the domain invariants from the project addendum, package boundaries. Run before stage acceptance, before archiving a change that alters product behavior, and after any change to a contract or data schema. ([bodasooqa/rootstock](https://github.com/bodasooqa/rootstock/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-auditor.md) · 2 copies)
- **spec-auditor**: WAP-Planer – unabhängige Katalogprüfung (Satz-für-Satz-Zuordnung des Beschriebs), Planprüfung (jede ID einem Paket und Testplan) und Etappen-Audit gegen laufende App, Tests und Belege. Ändert nichts. Nur auf Auftrag des Orchestrators. ([bubavanhalen/detachementsplaner-fdt](https://github.com/bubavanhalen/detachementsplaner-fdt/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-auditor.md))
- **spec-auditor**: Adversarial, read-only auditor that checks the current diff against the task's US# acceptance criteria — does the code actually do what the task asked, with tests that encode each criterion? Invoke from a pre-PR gate (e.g. /next-task §7) with the task ID in the prompt, so the agent that wrote the code is NOT the one grading completeness. Returns PASS / FAIL with file:line evidence. ([Chris-Graffagnino/creance](https://github.com/Chris-Graffagnino/creance/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-auditor.md))
- **spec-auditor**: Audits a diff against a CLM component's requirements and scenarios, read-only. Launch it with a component ID before opening the component's pull request. ([clunny/clm](https://github.com/clunny/clm/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/spec-auditor.md))
- **spec-auditor**: Skeptical, independent verifier of EE4525 design claims. Use proactively before ticking any PROGRESS.md phase, before claiming a spec passes, after power-descent steps, and before declaring the task done. It re-runs the tools itself and reports only real gaps. ([danieltyukov/tud-analog-cmos-design-2](https://github.com/danieltyukov/tud-analog-cmos-design-2/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-auditor.md))
- **spec-auditor**: Cross-checks the Clickco specification set for contradictions, gaps and unresolved decisions. Use once at project start, and again whenever a spec document changes. ([darshil320/Clicko](https://github.com/darshil320/Clicko/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-auditor.md))
- **spec-auditor**: Audits a working tree against a numbered spec and reports findings. Read-only: it never edits a file and never runs the tests. Invoke it explicitly after a run, with the spec path in the prompt. ([DaveedBalcher/spec-driven-development](https://github.com/DaveedBalcher/spec-driven-development/blob/HEAD/day-2-loops-and-your-environment/starter/agents/spec-auditor.md))
- **spec-auditor**: Spec-conformance auditor and free investigator. Re-confirms machine-flagged AC gaps AND investigates the whole spec for off-matrix failures — dormant mechanisms, swallowed errors, hollow code, broken end-to-end flows. Read-only — never edits. Distinct from the test-quarantined `auditor`. ([davidel8698-stack/apex](https://github.com/davidel8698-stack/apex/blob/HEAD/framework/agents/spec-auditor.md))
- **spec-auditor**: Read-only. Diffs what the repo claims against what the code does — per spec phase, per DSL construct, per MAGIK_* code — and reports the overstatements by file. Writes nothing. Use before a release, before trusting a doc, or when a plan is built on paperwork. ([developerz-ai/magik](https://github.com/developerz-ai/magik/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-auditor.md))
- **spec-auditor**: Use this agent during fable-conductor's Phase 5 (Finalize) for a blinded cold-read of a diff against a spec's acceptance criteria — before Fable's whole-branch final review. Dispatch it with ONLY specPath + diffRef + repoRoot: no task reports, no implementer notes, no plan rationale. The blindness is the point — this agent's value comes from having no access to anyone's account of what was intended or why, only the spec and the code as they exist. Examples: &lt;example&gt; Context: A fable-conductor stream has finished Phase 4 execution and is entering Phase 5 finalize. user: "all tasks in the plan… ([DMokong/claude-plugins](https://github.com/DMokong/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/fable-conductor/agents/spec-auditor.md))
- **spec-auditor**: Audit the local OpenAPI spec against the upstream Frontapp API to detect drift, missing endpoints, field mismatches, and type discrepancies. ([dougborg/frontapp-openapi-client](https://github.com/dougborg/frontapp-openapi-client/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-auditor.md))
- **spec-auditor**: Audit the local OpenAPI spec at docs/statuspro-openapi.yaml for internal consistency — every $ref resolves, every endpoint referenced in helper code exists, parameter alignment matches. - **spec-auditor**: Performs an integrative audit of the complete Flow 1 spec (Problem Statement, Requirements, BDD, Risks, Questions) to verify coherence and readiness for Flow 2 (Plan). Never fixes. ([EffortlessMetrics/demo-swarm](https://github.com/EffortlessMetrics/demo-swarm/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-auditor.md))
- **spec-auditor**: Read-only full, scoped, or Git-diff specification auditor with retained evidence and resumable criterion-level coverage. ([emilhornlund/agent-orchestrator](https://github.com/emilhornlund/agent-orchestrator/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/spec-auditor.md))
- **spec-auditor**: Audits implemented code against its specification and reports which acceptance criteria are met, deviated from or missing, plus requirements with no implementation and code with no requirement. - **spec-auditor**: Checks whether a WS-n workstream is actually dispatchable — does its owning spec satisfy the seven-point contract in work_plan.md §1, and is the work OWNER-GATE? - **spec-auditor**: Auditor della spec in L2, lanciato da tollgate:spec-audit con il percorso del brief. Un solo ciclo, da un solo punto di vista, con le dieci lenti; sola lettura, chiude con una riga JSON. ([ginz0ls/tollgate](https://github.com/ginz0ls/tollgate/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-auditor.md))
- **spec-auditor**: Independently re-judges whether an implementation actually reached the spec, reading only the spec text and the current code — never the implementer's report. - **spec-author**: Specification authoring agent. It turns the raw intent into one draft spec file under specs/ in the interaction mode of the parent ticket. Not for implementation plans, ADRs, PR descriptions or code. Run this agent on the gemini-4-hydrogen-medium model. ([crewrig/crewrig](https://github.com/crewrig/crewrig/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/spec-author.md) · ★24)

Отдельная сила — кросс-документная согласованность сьюта (фантомные ссылки, двойное владение, противоречия между спеками), которую пофайловое ревью пропускает. - **spec-critic**: Adversarial critic for a DRAFT SPEC, before any code is written.
